# Foundry decision and attempt log

This is the durable history for the reference-guided icon foundry. Backfilled on **5 September 2026** from the conversation, repository documents and saved experiment receipts. Exact earlier timestamps are unavailable; entries use stable IDs instead of invented times. Historical results are retained even when a later decision supersedes them.

**Latest reconciled state:** $5.72662485 spent of the $50 round ceiling; $44.27337515 remaining; no reservation outstanding. Fourteen paid generation/review experiments, no qualified pilot, no training job or upload, no released library. Latest engine run: 1,394 tests in 99 files, zero skipped; root lint and typecheck pass. These establish software checks, not professional icon quality.

## How to maintain this log

- Append each material decision and every attempt, including rejected, interrupted, misconfigured and zero-cost attempts, before ending the work session. Record intent before a paid call; append the actual outcome afterward.
- Give entries stable IDs, date, hypothesis or decision, rationale, alternatives, actual configuration, evidence paths, outcome, cost or unknown charge, and next action. Identify generated, retrieved, reconstructed and host-repaired artifacts separately.
- Record reservations before spending. Settle against provider receipts, including failed calls and reviews. Unknown charges retain their reservation and stop further paid work until reconciled. Never bill an old generation again when only reviewing it.
- Keep original artifacts immutable. Corrections are new entries with parent IDs and reasons; superseded decisions stay readable. Do not rewrite a failed attempt as a success after repairing its program.
- Retain small summaries and receipts under `docs/log/`. Large or third-party originals remain in their existing ignored directories; link them and record hashes. Hashes detect changed/missing evidence, but are not an asset backup. Do not move the corpus or stage vendor bundles.
- At handoff, reconcile the log against the budget ledger and new artifact directories. If evidence is unavailable, say so. Do not reconstruct missing scores, costs or chronology from guesses.

The [round-one evidence snapshot](log/foundry-round-1.json) retains configurations, costs, actual audit results and hashes for each run's files, even if local `.staging` files later disappear. Full plans and source discussions remain in the linked documents below.

## Decisions

All entries below were backfilled on 2026-09-05. “Implemented” describes the relevant mechanism, not completion of the foundry goal.

| ID | Decision and status | Rationale, alternative and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D001 | **Active:** artwork must be 100% AI-generated, including repairs and optical masters. | User clarification superseded hybrid human authoring and mandatory human artwork approval. Inspection can evaluate the system; manual drawing cannot qualify the production result. [Research](plans/reference-guided-foundry.research.md). |
| D002 | **Active:** programs, pinned styles and generated masters are authoritative; SVG remains delivery format. Figma/Glyphs are optional downstream tools. | Neither editor solves generation quality. Glyphs 4 icon support was researched, but native export automation remains unverified; its inspected local trial was expired. Defer the bridge until a named consumer needs it. [Tool decision](plans/reference-guided-foundry.research.md). |
| D003 | **Active:** prove drawing and evaluation quality before Studio style selection, batch jobs or library releases. | First-principles audit superseded the discovery map's infrastructure-first sequence. KISS/YAGNI; use existing seams and no new service. [Audit](plans/reference-guided-foundry.audit.md), [plan](plans/reference-guided-foundry.md). |
| D004 | **Implemented:** select one immutable style/master context for generation, parts, reference images, replay and review. | Scattered house defaults allowed drift. Selected styles disable implicit house construction; legacy calls retain their default path. Unsupported canvases fail rather than silently rescale. [Implementation notes](plans/reference-guided-foundry.notes.md). |
| D005 | **Active:** model does not emit arbitrary path/control geometry. | Reuse admitted parts and host-owned constructions. Direct free cubics, local stroke roles and cap controls remain unsupported; training cannot add compiler expressivity. [Craft breakdown](references/blode-central-craft.md). |
| D006 | **Active:** preserve acquisition scope and provenance; extracted product assets remain analysis-only unless admitted. | Public assets do not establish a complete library or conditioning grant. Keep separate cohorts for Central matches, Lucide, brands and unclassified samples. [Collections](references/product-icon-collections.md). |
| D007 | **Implemented:** admit the actual delivered pair using exact replay, fresh lint, finish/style identity and pair geometry. | Primitive-only output may qualify; inserting a part is not quality proof. Rescue must not hide original costs or mismatched extents. A005–A007 motivated the pair check. [Notes](plans/reference-guided-foundry.notes.md). |
| D008 | **Active:** automated judge scores are exploratory until the instrument qualifies. | Weight controls failed the frozen rejection threshold. No RL against this judge, no professional-quality claim from 10/10 scores. Pilot gate requires evidence for 24 concepts and frozen splits; its collector is not implemented end to end. [Audit](plans/reference-guided-foundry.audit.md). |
| D009 | **Active:** $50 is the cumulative ceiling for this round. | User authorization covers bounded experiments and conditional training, not an independently renewed budget per turn. Preserve failed-attempt charges, unknown spend and reservations. [Receipt snapshot](log/foundry-round-1.json). |
| D010 | **Implemented:** include named reference images before drawing, hide unusable part tools, expose bounded effort/output settings. | Early empty fills and token exhaustion showed that more steps alone were insufficient. A008 exposed a plan/execution mismatch; forwarding is now fixed. |
| D011 | **Exploratory:** test real curved Blode components and external-badge composition. | Source study showed continuity, counters and optical redrawing missing from crude primitive compositions. This changes several inputs together; it does not isolate causation or demonstrate original source contours. A008–A011 retain this distinction. |
| D012 | **Implemented:** serialize part multipliers as `scale`; retain absolute `size`; preserve explicit positions directly. | A009–A011 exposed two host replay bugs. Derivative programs reproduce original SVGs without manual artwork edits. Fractional scale, paint, rotation/reflection and legacy-size regression checks pass. |
| D013 | **Deferred, authorized if needed:** train only after the representation and demonstrations are reliable. | Train reference/context → construction choices; keep related concepts, aliases, finishes and near-duplicate geometry on one side of the evaluation split. Compare against an unchanged base-model baseline. Rejected drawings are not positive targets. No provider or training price selected. [Training decision](plans/foundry-training-decision.md). |
| D014 | **Active:** maintain this append-only history. | Explicit user request to log all decisions and past attempts. Add corrections and superseding entries; preserve original evidence and cost history. |

## Open questions and next evidence

- No generated library or style is `pilot-proven`. Fix contour construction and badge-specific negative space; test unseen concepts before scaling.
- The critic needs qualified controls for subtle craft and semantics. One gross-weight experiment is insufficient.
- Reference acquisition is bounded; full native Granola, complete Cursor/OpenAI/X collections and conditioning eligibility remain unestablished.
- Optical masters, generated family anchors, complete holdout closure, durable library jobs and release export remain unfinished. Compiler identity is recorded, but an archived executable compiler bundle is still outstanding.
- No training was launched. Revisit D013 when good replayable demonstrations and a priced experiment exist.

## Coverage boundary

This backfill covers the decisions and attempts supported by this foundry conversation, its documents and all eleven entries of `foundry-round-1/budget.json`. Older unrelated `.staging/eve-*` and `reach-10` campaigns are not relabeled as round-one experiments. Their complete historical chronology has not been reconstructed. Existing detailed notes remain historical evidence; old test counts or interim statuses refer to their recorded stage, not the latest state above.

- **D019:** Use claude-code-search for historical backfill, following the user's instruction. The earlier boundary remains above as the original statement. Historical claims are not silently promoted to current facts.
- **R010:** ccs CLI combined/Cursor exports failed on invalid dates. A read-only library export preserved 94 dates as unknown. A first legacy search used the displayed `icon/forge` label and returned zero; the actual `icon-forge` path recovered seven more sessions. No history store or ccs code was modified. H001–H014 retain the material findings and cost discrepancies.
- **D020:** Add filled-shape `union` and `subtract` using pinned Paper.js 0.12.18 in the existing tools layer. Store replayable operand recipes, not model-authored raw paths. Compute before mutation; reject empty results, open paths and outlined recipes. Union overlapping cutters before subtracting to avoid even-odd XOR islands. Transform operands before composition; post-composition transforms are explicitly refused. This is not a general clearance offset or stroke-expansion implementation.
- **D021:** Preserve admitted part contours under explicit `partGeometry: source`; snap placement but do not independently snap the source's cubic handles. Default grid behavior stays unchanged. Source mode creates a different immutable style revision. Move static Spec definitions into a dependency-free tools module so the narrow public spec import does not load Paper.

This is host-constructed capability evidence, not AI generation or a 10/10 result. No new provider calls; round total remains $5.72662485 with no holds. Artifact hashes are retained in [the receipt](log/boolean-construction-2026-09-05.json).

That is evidence to investigate reference-declared local weight roles, not permission to invent arbitrary model widths. No local-weight feature or training run has been implemented in this stage. Preserve the no-free-path invariant and measure per-element ink bounds if adding optical weight support.
